# provenance/evidence/

Generated by mkmanifest.py from the MANIFEST of each directory: core-A/, core-B/, fakestack/, kit-relocation/, prog-gen/, repin/, rt-setshadow/, rt-wrappers/, s11/, srcreloc/, startup-gen/, tmodel/, yh03/. Commit ids in these records are kept as the runs wrote them; the commits they name that are not in the released history are preserved in the internal record.

## kit-relocation/

| file | kind | role |
|---|---|---|
| kit_relocate.py | script | the script of record: copies libreac/fuzz_decode's released plain / UBSan lib (K1), moves every image value to this unit's binaries (K2), adds second-donor libc walks when linked (K3); its docstring states each step and control. It is the shared libdiginet kit relocation script as run for the tables here (sha256 967abd6019c3) with three changes: its docstrings name no internal path and no longer name a summary file it never wrote; the roadapt path it rewords in donor comments is written as two adjacent string literals, which Python joins into the same constant; and the evidence path its notices name is kit-relocation/values.tsv (this directory and table were renamed from k2/k2-values.tsv, and the 159 notices in lib/ that cite it were changed the same way). Against the first script of record (sha256 6335e2012e0c) it adds rule (g) and names decode's two harness check functions (check_round_trip, check_braid) as harness files that are never kept; against 8f17a31858b9 it dates every notice it writes from --month (YYYY-MM, no day, no agent, one notice on each file whose text differs from the donor's) and adds rule (h). Step (d'): a YH03 data cell that no donor symbol covers moves by content, when the donor bytes from the cell to the next donor symbol (8 to 64 bytes) occur exactly once in this unit's loaded image. On this unit it moved one cell, plain src0_D_debruijn32: 0x10480 -> 0x10460. The ubsan cell moved by the existing rule (0x10488 -> 0x10500), which equals the unique content match. Step (g): a decimal address inside a driver string constant (YL0v's rodata_*_src1 lemmas and the YL0w / YL0x / YL0y walks that read them) moves with its whole NUL-terminated string, which must occur once in this unit's image. On this unit it moved 66365 -> 66389 ("cli: cannot open %s\n" + 19) and 66388 -> 66412 ("cli: read error on %s\n" + 21) in Reusable/YL0v.v, YL0w.v, YL0w_cfw.v and their U/ twins. Step (h): a kept YH03 string cell src0_D_str_<X> moves by the bytes the kit states at it; this unit's kit keeps no src0_D_str_* cell (cells.tsv), so rule (h) moved nothing and this script's lib/ is byte-identical to the one of sha256 52e5bc690855, which the earlier runs of this kit used. |
| values.tsv | evidence | every value it changed: file, line, old, new, how (and each file it did not copy, with the reason); the run writes it as <work>/k2-values.tsv, and the notices of the lib/ files it changed cite it |
| conflicts.tsv | evidence | hex values the address tool also classes NON-ADDRESS, not applied (written as <work>/k2-conflicts.tsv) |
| cells.tsv | evidence | every kept YH03 cell: file, line, cell, value, the rule that set it and the measurement (written as <work>/k2-cells.tsv) |

- kit-relocation/: libreac/fuzz_decode's released plain / UBSan kit, copied into this unit and moved to its binaries.
- <path> <kind> <sha256> <role>, paths relative to this directory.
- Run for this unit: `kit_relocate.py --project libdiginet --unit fuzz_transport --month 2026-10 --donor-ref bf7779f81ed764787b77895d2f5c0db2627f33f3 --kit-ref 25e3d17bdb5059606131377a3722d585415ab5d6 --plain <fuzz_transport.plain pin bb20ce415827>:<unstripped twin c2d42d895415> --ubsan <fuzz_transport.ubsan pin af7b86991906>:<unstripped twin c53d4b20cfc1> --out lib --work <dir> --git <hub> --pac <port_addr_check 5033540ba83b> --decode-bin <libreac fuzz_decode pins> --donor-bin <second donors' pins>`.
- The pins are the builds of the port commit 835f336c + c47f6290 (plain bb20ce415827592a9ab28fd0bd5b242215eb3ff03b629e12a4723cc6f44ace56, ubsan af7b86991906e2fd59d929595aaaf239f5f5805109f480ef9ef99e286a97840d); the three tables are that run's (K2: 3443 changes, 4 hex conflicts not applied), made by the script as run (sha256 967abd6019c3c487afc3a6e79363e50afce17d17b1d11be8de82eaede6e4a3eb) and copied here by repin/repin_reloc.py all at the re-pin commit f6acfcb0c1b8. Four rows, cells.tsv:37 and :79 and values.tsv:3340 and :3388, name the YH03 cell src0_D_oft_aligned_copy_empty, its name after the rename that took a codename out of the identifier: the run wrote the earlier name, only the name changed, and the values are as the run wrote them.
- The donor ref is libreac/fuzz_decode as released after PR #413; the kit ref limits the copied files to the plain / UBSan kit of 25e3d17bdb50 (the files added since are the ASan kit). K3 added strlen from libreac/fuzz_disco (plain and ubsan; code equal, 2 constant-pool pairs with equal values).
- Verbatim originals, internal record commit 9648075ea42476f7cb3a8414aac2db46f395a174: evidence-1282bd206d88/k2/MANIFEST.md sha256 c4c015658c6514cc72b8644281857d2b5f58cff871650d90e4d0ffa0b57a6109; evidence-1282bd206d88/k2/k2_relocate.py sha256 967abd6019c3c487afc3a6e79363e50afce17d17b1d11be8de82eaede6e4a3eb.

## repin/

| file | kind | role |
|---|---|---|
| repin_reloc.py | script | the re-pin tool of record: `all` runs the kit relocation at the old and the new pins, carries the tree's post-relocation edits onto the new output (kit), moves every image numeral of the unit's own files by the rules of its docstring (own), and reruns ../srcreloc/relocate.py at the new pins (src); a run with any STOP writes nothing. The re-pin commit f6acfcb0c1b8 is its output. Shipped with its host path constants made options: --kit (required), --git and --src-unstripped (all), --pac, --decode-bin, --donor-bin and --k2-pythonpath (when all runs the kit relocation, i.e. without --k2-old / --k2-new); the kit relocation it runs is ../kit-relocation/kit_relocate.py, whose sha256 it checks, and it copies that run's tables to ../kit-relocation/{values,conflicts,cells}.tsv. Its rules are unchanged from the script as run (sha256 9f0426f8844a); it reads srcreloc's per-file list of the lib/ files from ../srcreloc/relocate-list.tsv, where the script as run read the lib/ rows of ../srcreloc/MANIFEST |
| codedelta.py | script | step 1 of the re-pin: every function of fuzz_transport's own program and of libdiginet, old pin vs new, plain and ubsan, compared by the fixed comparison kit (cmpkit 4bbd3a74be89, strictcmp2 16982e3ef42b); the kit directory is the required --kit argument (a constant in the script as run, sha256 824b71bc53d9). Rerun with the same inputs it prints codedelta.tsv as run byte for byte (checked) |
| codedelta.tsv | evidence | codedelta.py's output: one row per function and build with its verdict and the summary counts: plain 47 EQUAL, 15 EQUAL-mod-pcrel, 2 DIFFERENT; ubsan 29 EQUAL, 39 EQUAL-mod-pcrel, 2 DIFFERENT; the DIFFERENT ones are io_recv and receive in both builds. Header lines 1, 2 and 4 name the kit directory and the old twins by role, not by path |
| own-values.tsv | evidence | every image numeral of the unit's own files in the re-pin run of record (file, line, code\|comment, old, new, rule) |
| own-comments-left.tsv | evidence | the comment numerals that run left, each with the reason |

| source-citations.tsv | evidence | every line citation of fuzz_transport.c in the files the re-pin base brought, moved from the old source (sha256 044738ce0df1, 1215 lines) to the patched source (sha256 ae837185e625, 1218 lines): the map, and a byte check of each cited line |
| source-citations-integration.tsv | evidence | the citations the integration wrote with pre-re-pin line numbers, which source-citations.tsv does not cover, moved by the same map and checked the same way |

## srcreloc/

| file | kind | role |
|---|---|---|
| relocate.py | script | the script of record: copies the proved walks and contracts of the libdiginet src/ functions fuzz_transport reaches from fuzz_frame and fuzz_codec and moves every image numeral to fuzz_transport's images by symbol + offset (S0-S5 in its docstring); it writes relocate-list.tsv (its per-file list of the lib/ files), values.tsv, files.tsv and sample20.tsv. Shipped with its host path constants made options, --unstripped (required) and --pins (unless both --target-* are given), and without the unread who= / branch= fields of its donor tables; at the unit's pins its dry run prints what the script as run (sha256 4b4cb66ba33a) prints (checked); its per-file list of the lib/ files, the lib/ rows of MANIFEST in the script as run, is written to and read from relocate-list.tsv, a change in S5 only, which runs after the dry run ends |
| relocate-list.tsv | evidence | relocate.py's per-file list: one row per lib/ file it wrote (path relative to the unit folder, kind, sha256 as written, role, donor path@commit); relocate.py and ../repin/repin_reloc.py read it |
| values.tsv | evidence | every moved numeral (file line old new how) |
| files.tsv | evidence | function x build x file status, and the S1 equality rows |
| sample20.tsv | evidence | the 20 moved numerals of the hand cross-check |
| crosscheck.md | evidence | the hand cross-check of sample20.tsv against the ELF |

- relocate-list.tsv holds relocate.py's row of each lib/ file it wrote (its header names the columns); the sha256 of a row is the file as relocate.py wrote it. relocate.py and repin/repin_reloc.py read it; a later edit of the tree shows as a different sha256, and repin_reloc.py carries such edits onto a new output.
- The rows of this directory's files name them relative to this directory (relocate.py writes them relative to the unit folder), with the shipped files' sha256.
- The source-equality comparison that set the donors and TWIN_SHA: inputs/src-equality-v2.md of the internal record commit 9648075ea42476f7cb3a8414aac2db46f395a174 (sha256 c533185d0630ac0c197a5b1d6fd23b1035856eefa0c918b35128752ec0f6ad48). The verbatim originals of MANIFEST and relocate.py: evidence-1282bd206d88/srcreloc/ of the same record.

- yh03/: every cell of lib/Reusable/YH03.v and lib/U/Reusable/YH03.v measured against the unit's own pinned images, and checked again by a second tool.

The twins: the unstripped files of the same build, plain sha256 c2d42d89541589e1dcd45703807ba69a5b3ac1ed28c71d6c6a047187c33201b6 and ubsan sha256 c53d4b20cfc1e663ed016fafc60f7680f61ffa47ce31a65c8410c55926d04686.
- Cell kinds per file: 27 A, 15 D, 1 src0_vmlock (43). Neither file has a src0_D_str_* cell, so the string pass of yh03regen.py had no cell to check.
- Evidence kind: the cell values were written by kit-relocation/kit_relocate.py (K2, run on these pins) and brought into this tree by repin/repin_reloc.py at the re-pin commit f6acfcb0c1b8. measure_yh03.py and yh03regen.py wrote none of them: both check the values after they were written, and yh03regen.py is a second, separate checker.
- yh03regen.py v6.4 (sha256 1254e5c96a44d38256357a06b92992c960c339d45bfbe2a2d8505d47e8e4dbde), run from the tree root for the plain file: python3 yh03regen.py /dev/null proofs/libdiginet/fuzz_transport/lib/Reusable/YH03.v --pinned bin/fuzz_transport/fuzz_transport.plain --syms-elf unstripped/fuzz_transport/fuzz_transport.plain --elf bin/fuzz_transport/fuzz_transport.plain --pins projects/libdiginet/fuzz_transport/harness.yaml --proofs proofs/libdiginet/fuzz_transport --report yh03regen-plain.report; for the ubsan file the same with lib/U/Reusable/YH03.v and the .ubsan files.
- The measurement on these pins before the cell was renamed to src0_D_oft_aligned_copy_empty (86 OK, the same values): yh03-cells.tsv sha256 9fe559928e7e1bea92a6e61ea007a3007ec4d41b166c1d87dbbba1e7eac2b35a; its stdout is runs/yh03-measure-shipped-pins.stdout of the internal record commit 9648075ea42476f7cb3a8414aac2db46f395a174 (sha256 26d8f55e798938d6eff03d6103fd63143fc417809809a792f6f419e65fccf708).
- The measurement before the re-pin (pins 81d5585d6e5c / 9743b17ae4e0, 86 OK) is in evidence-1282bd206d88/yh03/ of the same record: evidence-1282bd206d88/yh03/yh03-cells.tsv sha256 becc06d1a6d49e0a293bf07c3b1c51d74b480f32dccf0171972004b789d42e9b; evidence-1282bd206d88/yh03/measure.stdout sha256 982725c2b2f0f6e8bcfb8219d7a965e2da428326aba1843855bd06abaee91bbd; evidence-1282bd206d88/yh03/MANIFEST sha256 1626f20bcd5fb50cd877b234e94073372efb14ebf72a05fb8f151ebf99391ad3.
